new_menu_btn = QPushButton(\"\u2795 New Menu\")\n        new_menu_btn.clicked.connect(self.new_menu)\n        tree_btn_layout.addWidget(new_menu_btn)\n        \n        new_folder_btn = QPushButton(\"\ud83d\udcc1 New Folder\")\n        new_folder_btn.clicked.connect(self.new_folder)\n        tree_btn_layout.addWidget(new_folder_btn)\n        \n        delete_btn = QPushButton(\"\ud83d\uddd1\ufe0f Delete\")\n        delete_btn.setObjectName(\"danger\")\n        delete_btn.clicked.connect(self.delete_item)\n        tree_btn_layout.addWidget(delete_btn)\n        \n        left_layout.addLayout(tree_btn_layout)\n        \n        splitter.addWidget(left_widget)\n        \n        # Right side - Editor\n        right_widget = QWidget()\n        right_layout = self.name_edit.setPlaceholderText(\"Menu name\")\n        form_layout.addRow(\"Name:\", self.name_edit)\n        \n        self.type_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.type_combo.addItems([\"Branch (submenu)\", \"Leaf (content window)\", \"Script (.py file)\"])\n        self.type_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_type_changed)\n        form_layout.addRow(\"Type:\", self.type_combo)\n        \n        editor_layout.addLayout(form_layout)\n        \n        # Leaf config\n        self.leaf_group = QGroupBox(\"Leaf Menu Configuration\")\n        leaf_layout = self.title_edit)\n        \n        self.content_edit = QLineEdit()\n        self.content_edit.setPlaceholderText(\"content.html\")\n        leaf_layout.addRow(\"Content File:\", self.content_edit)\n        \n        self.script_edit = QLineEdit()\n        self.script_edit.setPlaceholderText(\"lsof -i\")\n        leaf_layout.addRow(\"Window Script:\", self.script_edit)\n        \n        self.loop_spin = QSpinBox()\n        self.loop_spin.setRange(0, 3600)\n        self.loop_spin.setValue(0)\n        self.loop_spin.setSuffix(\" seconds\")\n        leaf_layout.addRow(\"Loop script_layout.addRow(\"Script Path:\", self.script_path_edit)\n        \n        self.venv_path_edit = QLineEdit()\n        self.venv_path_edit.setPlaceholderText(\"/home/user/.venv/myenv self.preview_label = QLabel(\"Content Preview:\")\n        editor_layout.addWidget(self.preview_label)\n        self.preview = QTextEdit()\n        self.preview.setReadOnly(True)\n \n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        \n        close_btn = QPushButton(\"Close\")\n        close_btn.clicked.connect(self.accept)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(close_btn)\n        \n        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)\n        \n        # Initially disable editor\n        self.editor_group.setEnabled(False)\n    \n    def refresh_tree(self):\n        \"\"\"Refresh the tree from disk\"\"\"\n        self.tree.clear()\n        \n        if not self.menus_dir.exists():\n            self.menus_dir.mkdir(parents=True)\n            return\n        \n        # Scan directories\n        for item_path in sorted(self.menus_dir.iterdir()):\n            if item_path.is_dir() and item_path.name != '__pycache__':\n                self._add_tree_item(self.tree.invisibleRootItem(), item_path)\n        \n        self.tree.expandAll()\n    \n    def _add_tree_item(self, parent, path):\n        \"\"\"Recursively add items to tree\"\"\"\n        item = QTreeWidgetItem(parent)\n        item.setText(0, path.name)\n        item.setData(0, Qt.UserRole, str(path))\n        \n        # Determine type\n        config_file = path / \"config.json\"\n        if config_file.exists():\n            item.setIcon(0, self.style().standardIcon(self.style().SP_DialogOpenButton))\n            item.setToolTip(0, \"Leaf menu (click to open window)\")\n        elif any(f.suffix == '.py' for f in path.iterdir() if f.is_file()):\n            item.setIcon(0, self.style().standardIcon(self.style().SP_FileIcon))\n            item.setToolTip(0, \"Script menu\")\n        else:\n            item.setIcon(0, self.style().standardIcon(self.style().SP_DirIcon))\n            item.setToolTip(0, \"Branch menu (submenu)\")\n        \n        # Recurse into subdirectories\n        if path.is_dir():\n            for subpath in sorted(path.iterdir()):\n                if subpath.is_dir() and subpath.name != '__pycache__':\n                    self._add_tree_item(item, subpath)\n    \n    def on_tree_item_clicked(self, item, column):\n        \"\"\"Handle tree item selection\"\"\"\n        path_str = item.data(0, Qt.UserRole)\n        if not path_str:\n            return\n        \n        path = Path(path_str)\n        self.current_item = path\n        \n        self.editor_group.setEnabled(True)\n        self.name_edit.setText(path.name)\n        \n        # Determine type and load config\n        config_file = path / \"config.json\"\n        py_files = list(path.glob(\"*.py\"))\n        \n        if config_file.exists():\n            # Leaf menu\n            self.type_combo.setCurrentIndex(1)\n            try:\n                with open(config_file) as f:\n                    config = json.load(f)\n                self.title_edit.setText(config.get(\"title\", \"\"))\n                self.content_edit.setText(config.get(\"windowContent\", \"\"))\n                self.script_edit.setText(config.get(\"windowScript\", \"\"))\n                self.loop_spin.setValue(config.get(\"loop\", 0))\n                \n                # Load script-specific fields if present\n                self.script_path_edit.setText(config.get(\"scriptPath\", \"\"))\n                self.venv_path_edit.setText(config.get(\"venvPath\", \"\"))\n                \n                # Show preview\n                content_file = path / config.get(\"windowContent\", \"\")\n                if content_file.exists():\n                    try:\n                        with open(content_file) as f:\n                            self.preview.setText(f.read()[:1000])\n                    except:\n                        self.preview.setText(\"Unable to read content file\")\n                else:\n                    self.preview.setText(\"Content file not found\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                self.preview.setText(\"Error loading config: \" + str(e))\n        elif py_files:\n            # Script menu\n            self.type_combo.setCurrentIndex(2)\n            self.preview.setText(\"Python script: \" + py_files[0].name)\n        else:\n            # Branch menu\n            self.type_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            self.leaf_group.setVisible(False)\n            self.script_group.setVisible(False)\n            self.preview.setText(\"Branch menu (contains sub-items)\")\n    \n    def on_type_changed(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle type change - show/hide appropriate groups\"\"\"\n        self.leaf_group.setVisible(index == 1)  # Show only for Leaf\n        self.script_group.setVisible(index == 2)  # Show only for Script\n        self.preview_label.setVisible(index != 2)  # Hide preview for Script\n        self.preview.setVisible(index != 2)  # Hide preview for Script\n    \n    def new_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Create a new top-level menu\"\"\"\n        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, \"New Menu\", \"Enter menu name:\")\n        if ok and name:\n            new_path = self.menus_dir / name\n            try:\n                new_path.mkdir(parents=True)\n                self.refresh_tree()\n            except Exception as e:\n                QMessageBox.critical(self, \"Error\", \"Failed to create menu:\\n\" + str(e))\n    \n    def new_folder(self):\n        \"\"\"Create a new folder in selected menu\"\"\"\n        current = self.tree.currentItem()\n        if not current:\n            QMessageBox.information(self, \"Info\", \"Select a menu first\")\n            return\n        \n        path_str = current.data(0, Qt.UserRole)\n        if not path_str:\n            return\n        \n        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, \"New Folder\", \"Enter folder name:\")\n        if ok and name:\n            new_path = Path(path_str) / name\n            try:\n                new_path.mkdir(parents=True)\n                \n                # Create default config.json for leaf menu\n                config = {\n                    \"title\": name,\n                    \"windowContent\": \"content.html\"\n                }\n                with open(new_path / \"config.json\", 'w') as f:\n                    json.dump(config, f, indent=2)\n                \n                # Create sample content\n                with open(new_path / \"content.html\", 'w') as f:\n                    f.write(\"<h1>\" + name + \"</h1>\\n<p>Content for \" + name + \"</p>\\n\")\n                \n                self.refresh_tree()\n            except Exception as e:\n                QMessageBox.critical(self, \"Error\", \"Failed to create folder:\\n\" + str(e))\n    \n    def delete_item(self):\n        \"\"\"Delete selected menu item\"\"\"\n        current = self.tree.currentItem()\n        if not current:\n            QMessageBox.information(self, \"Info\", \"Select an item to delete\")\n            return\n        \n        path_str = current.data(0, Qt.UserRole)\n        if not path_str:\n            return\n        \n        reply = QMessageBox.question(\n            self,\n            \"Confirm Delete\",\n            \"Delete '\" + current.text(0) + \"' and all its contents?\",\n            QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No\n        )\n        \n        if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:\n            try:\n                shutil.rmtree(path_str)\n                self.refresh_tree()\n                self.editor_group.setEnabled(False)\n                self.preview.clear()\n            except Exception as e:\n                QMessageBox.critical(self, \"Error\", \"Failed to delete:\\n\" + str(e))\n    \n    def save_changes(self):\n        \"\"\"Save changes to config.json\"\"\"\n        if not self.current_item:\n            return\n        \n        config_file = self.current_item / \"config.json\"\n        type_index = self.type_combo.currentIndex()\n        \n        try:\n            config = {}\n            \n            if type_index == 0:  # Branch\n                config[\"type\"] = \"branch\"\n            elif type_index == 1:  # Leaf\n                config = {\n                    \"title\": self.title_edit.text(),\n                    \"windowContent\": self.content_edit.text(),\n                    \"windowScript\": self.script_edit.text(),\n                    \"loop\": self.loop_spin.value(),\n                    \"looptype\": \"second\"\n                }\n                # Remove empty values\n                config = {k: v for k, v in config.items() if v}\n Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        # Initialize Panel API server (before setup_ui so gadgets can access it)\n        self.api = PanelAPI(port=8080)\n        self.api.start()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n\n[-- File truncated: 9414 chars remaining (54.1%). Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager and store as instance variable for config access\n        if not hasattr(self, 'gadget_manager') or self.gadget_manager is None:\n            self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Debug: print how many gadgets we're loading\n        enabled = self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Loading {len(enabled)} gadgets\")\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets - pass panel reference so gadgets can access API\n        for gadget in enabled:\n            print(f\"[Panel] Adding gadget: {gadget.get_name()} with icon {gadget.get_icon()}\")\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Make button visible and styled\n            btn.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 8px;\n                    font-size: 14px;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #555555;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications and API access\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n        \n        # Force layout update\n        self.gadgets_frame.update()\n        self.gadgets_frame.show()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Gadgets loaded: {list(self._gadget_buttons.keys())}\")\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        # Initialize Panel API server (before setup_ui so gadgets can access it)\n        self.api = PanelAPI(port=8080)\n        self.api.start()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager and store as instance variable for config access\n        if not hasattr(self, 'gadget_manager') or self.gadget_manager is None:\n            self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Debug: print how many gadgets we're loading\n        enabled = self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Loading {len(enabled)} gadgets\")\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets - pass panel reference so gadgets can access API\n        for gadget in enabled:\n            print(f\"[Panel] Adding gadget: {gadget.get_name()} with icon {gadget.get_icon()}\")\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Make button visible and styled\n            btn.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 8px;\n                    font-size: 14px;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #555555;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications and API access\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n        \n        # Force layout update\n        self.gadgets_frame.update()\n        self.gadgets_frame.show()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Gadgets loaded: {list(self._gadget_buttons.keys())}\")\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def closeEvent(self, event):\n        \"\"\"Clean up on close - stop the API server\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, 'api') and self.api:\n            self.api.stop()\n        event.accept()\n", "sessionId": 338, "rowId": 234, "timestamp": 1784980095.8675306, "date": "2026-07-25", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager and store as instance variable for config access\n        if not hasattr(self, 'gadget_manager') or self.gadget_manager is None:\n            self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Debug: print how many gadgets we're loading\n        enabled = self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Loading {len(enabled)} gadgets\")\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets - pass panel reference so gadgets can access API\n        for gadget in enabled:\n            print(f\"[Panel] Adding gadget: {gadget.get_name()} with icon {gadget.get_icon()}\")\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Make button visible and styled\n            btn.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 8px;\n                    font-size: 14px;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #555555;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications and API access\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n        \n        # Force layout update\n        self.gadgets_frame.update()\n        self.gadgets_frame.show()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Gadgets loaded: {list(self._gadget_buttons.keys())}\")\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        # Connect signal to reload menus when changes are saved\n        dialog.menus_changed.connect(self.reload_menus)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def reload_menus(self):\n        \"\"\"Reload menus from disk and refresh the menu button\"\"\"\n        # Clear and rebuild the menus menu\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def closeEvent(self, event):\n        \"\"\"Clean up on close - stop the API server\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, 'api') and self.api:\n            self.api.stop()\n        event.accept()\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 338, "rowId": 235, "timestamp": 1784980149.5663044, "date": "2026-07-25", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 39346, "response_tokens": 3681} -->

Scan each directory in Menus as a top-level menu\n        for menu_dir in sorted(self.menus_dir.iterdir()):\n            if menu_dir.is_dir() and menu_dir.name != '__pycache__':\n                menu_data = self._scan_menu_directory(menu_dir)\n                if menu_data:\n                    menus.append(menu_data)\n        \n        return menus\n    \n    def _scan_menu_directory(self, directory):\n        \"\"\"Recursively scan a menu directory\"\"\"\n        items = []\n        \n        for item_path in sorted(directory.iterdir()):\n            if item_path.name == '__pycache__':\n                continue\n            \n            if item_path.is_dir():\n                # Check if this subfolder has config.json (leaf menu)\n                config_file = item_path / \"config.json\"\n                if config_file.exists():\n                    # Leaf menu - add as clickable item\n                    try:\n                        with open(config_file) as f:\n                            config = json.load(f)\n                        items.append({\n                            \"name\": item_path.name,\n                            \"type\": \"leaf\",\n                            \"config\": config,\n                            \"path\": item_path\n                        })\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"Error loading config {config_file}: {e}\")\n                else:\n                    # Branch menu - recurse\n                    submenu = self._scan_menu_directory(item_path)\n                    if submenu:\n                        submenu[\"name\"] = item_path.name\n                        submenu[\"type\"] = \"branch\"\n                        items.append(submenu)\n            elif item_path.suffix == '.py' and item_path.name != '__init__.py':\n                # Python script - add as runnable script\n                items.append({\n                    \"name\": item_path.stem,\n                    \"type\": \"script\",\n                    \"path\": str(item_path)\n                })\n        \n        # Return as branch menu if has items\n        if items:\n            return {\n                \"name\": directory.name,\n                \"type\": \"branch\",\n                \"items\": items\n            }\n        \n        return None\n    \n    def _run_script(self, script_path):\n        \"\"\"Run a menu script with run() function\"\"\"\n        try:\n            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"menu_script\", script_path)\n            module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n            spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n            if hasattr(module, 'run'):\n                # Pass parent window to script\n                module.run(self.parent)\n            else:\n                print(f\"No run() function in {script_path}\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error running script {script_path}: {e}\")\n            QMessageBox.critical(\n                self.parent,\n                \"Error\",\n                f\"Failed to run script:\\n{e}\"\n            )\n    \n    def _open_leaf_menu(self, menu_data):\n        \"\"\"Show window with content from folder's config.json\"\"\"\n        config = menu_data.get(\"config\", {})\n        path = menu_data.get(\"path\")\n        \n        if not path:\n            return\n        \n        # Use longer delay to let menu close first (prevents freeze)\n        self._pending_dialog = (config, path)\n        QTimer.singleShot(100, self._show_pending_dialog)\n    \n    def _show_pending_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Show the pending dialog\"\"\"\n        if self._pending_dialog:\n            config, path = self._pending_dialog\n            self._show_leaf_dialog(config, path)\n            self._pending_dialog = None\n    \n    def _show_leaf_dialog(self, config, path):\n        \"\"\"Actually show the leaf dialog\"\"\"\n        # Get settings from config\n        content_file = config.get('windowContent', '')\n        script_cmd = config.get('windowScript', '')\n        loop_interval = config.get('loop', 0)\n        looptype = config.get('looptype', 'second')\n        title = config.get('title', path.name)\n        \n        # Create window\n        dialog = QDialog(self.parent)\n        dialog.setWindowTitle(title)\n        dialog.setGeometry(100, 100, 600, 400)\n        \n        # Dark theme for all leaf menus\n        if script_cmd:\n            # Terminal-like for script output\n            dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QDialog { background-color: #1a1a1a; }\n                QTextEdit { \n                    background-color: #000000; \n                    color: #00ff00; \n                    border: 1px solid #333333;\n                    border-radius: 3px;\n                    padding: 10px;\n                    font-family: monospace;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                }\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: #ffffff;\n                    border: 1px solid #555555;\n                    padding: 10px 20px;\n                    border-radius: 4px;\n                    font-weight: bold;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #5a5a5a;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n        else:\n            # <!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMenu system for KosDWM PyQt5\n\nAuto-generates menus from directory structure in ~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/\nMatches original TkInter behavior exactly\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import QMenu, QAction, QMessageBox, QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QTextEdit, QPushButton, QHBoxLayout\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\n\n\nclass MenuManager:\n    \"\"\"\n    Manages dynamic menus for KosDWM - auto-generated from directory structure\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.menus_dir = Path.home() / \".config\" / \"KosDWM\" / \"Menus\"\n        self._pending_dialog = None\n    \n    def load_menus(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all menus from directory structure\"\"\"\n        menus = []\n        \n        if not self.menus_dir.exists():\n            return menus\n        \n        # Scan each directory in Menus as a top-level menu\n        for menu_dir in sorted(self.menus_dir.iterdir()):\n            if menu_dir.is_dir() and menu_dir.name != '__pycache__':\n                menu_data = self._scan_menu_directory(menu_dir)\n                if menu_data:\n                    menus.append(menu_data)\n        \n        return menus\n    \n    def _scan_menu_directory(self, directory):\n        \"\"\"Recursively scan a menu directory\"\"\"\n        items = []\n        \n        for item_path in sorted(directory.iterdir()):\n            if item_path.name == '__pycache__':\n                continue\n            \n            if item_path.is_dir():\n                # Check if this subfolder has config.json\n                config_file = item_path / \"config.json\"\n                if config_file.exists():\n                    try:\n                        with open(config_file) as f:\n                            config = json.load(f)\n                        \n                        # Check if it's a script type config\n                        if config.get(\"type\") == \"script\":\n                            # Script menu with config\n                            script_path = config.get(\"scriptPath\", \"\")\n                            venv_path = config.get(\"venvPath\", \"\")\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"script\",\n                                \"path\": script_path,\n                                \"venv\": venv_path,\n                                \"config\": config\n                            })\n                        else:\n                            # Leaf menu - add as clickable item\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"leaf\",\n                                \"config\": config,\n                                \"path\": item_path\n                            })\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"Error loading config {config_file}: {e}\")\n                else:\n                    # def _add_tree_item(self, parent, path):\n        \"\"\"Recursively add items to tree\"\"\"\n        item = QTreeWidgetItem(parent)\n        item.setText(0, path.name)\n        item.setData(0, path = Path(path_str)\n        self.current_item = path\n        \n        self.editor_group.setEnabled(True)\n        self.name_edit.setText(path.name)\n        \n        # Determine type and load else:\n                    # Leaf menu\n                    self.type_combo.setCurrentIndex(1)\n                    self.title_edit.setText(config.get(\"title\", \"\"))\n                    self.content_edit.setText(config.get(\"windowContent\", \"\"))\n                    self.script_edit.setText(config.get(\"windowScript\", \"\"))\n                    self.loop_spin.setValue(config.get(\"loop\", 0))\n                    \n                    # Show preview\n                    content_file = path / config.get(\"windowContent\", \"\")\n self.type_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            self.leaf_group.setVisible(False)\n            self.script_group.setVisible(False)\n            self.xdg_group.setVisible(False)\n            self.preview.setText(\"Branch menu (contains sub-items)\")\n    \n    def on_type_changed(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle type change - show/hide appropriate groups\"\"\"\n        # index: 0=Branch, 1=Leaf, 2=Script, 3=xdgmenumaker\n        self.leaf_group.setVisible(index == 1)  # Show only for Leaf\n        self.script_group.setVisible(index == 2)  # Show only for Script\n        self.xdg_group.setVisible(index == 3)  # Show only for xdgmenumaker\n        self.preview_label.setVisible(index != 2 and index != 3)  # Hide preview for Script/xdgmenumaker\n        self.preview.setVisible(index != 2 and index != 3)  # Hide preview for Script/xdgmenumaker\n    \n    def new_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Create a new top-level menu\"\"\"\n        name, ok = except Exception as e:\n                QMessageBox.critical(self, \"Error\", \"Failed to create folder:\\n\" + str(e))\n    \n    def delete_item(self):\n        \"\"\"Delete selected menu \n            if type_index == 0:  # Branch\n                config[\"type\"] = \"branch\"\n            elif type_index == 1:  # Leaf\n                config = {\n                    \"title\": #!/usr/bin/env python3
> """
> Menu system for KosDWM PyQt5
> 
> Auto-generates menus from directory structure in ~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/
> Matches original TkInter behavior exactly
> """
>

> import subprocess
> import os

> from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer
>

>     Manages dynamic menus for KosDWM - auto-generated fr...

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMenu system for KosDWM PyQt5\n\nAuto-generates menus from directory structure in ~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/\nMatches original TkInter behavior exactly\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import QMenu, QAction, QMessageBox, QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QTextEdit, QPushButton, QHBoxLayout\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\n\n\nclass MenuManager:\n    \"\"\"\n    Manages dynamic menus for KosDWM - auto-generated from directory structure\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.menus_dir = Path.home() / \".config\" / \"KosDWM\" / \"Menus\"\n        self._pending_dialog = None\n    \n    def load_menus(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all menus from directory structure\"\"\"\n        menus = []\n        \n        if not self.menus_dir.exists():\n            return menus\n        \n        # Scan each directory in Menus as a top-level menu\n        for menu_dir in sorted(self.menus_dir.iterdir()):\n            if menu_dir.is_dir() and menu_dir.name != '__pycache__':\n                menu_data = self._scan_menu_directory(menu_dir)\n                if menu_data:\n                    menus.append(menu_data)\n        \n        return menus\n    \n    def _scan_menu_directory(self, directory):\n        \"\"\"Recursively scan a menu directory\"\"\"\n        items = []\n        \n        for item_path in sorted(directory.iterdir()):\n            if item_path.name == '__pycache__':\n                continue\n            \n            if item_path.is_dir():\n                # Check if this subfolder has config.json\n                config_file = item_path / \"config.json\"\n                if config_file.exists():\n                    try:\n                        with open(config_file) as f:\n                            config = json.load(f)\n                        \n                        # Check if it's a script type config\n                        if config.get(\"type\") == \"script\":\n                            # Script menu with config\n                            script_path = config.get(\"scriptPath\", \"\")\n                            venv_path = config.get(\"venvPath\", \"\")\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"script\",\n                                \"path\": script_path,\n                                \"venv\": venv_path,\n                                \"config\": config\n                            })\n                        else:\n                            # Leaf menu - add as clickable item\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"leaf\",\n                                \"config\": config,\n                                \"path\": item_path\n                            })\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"Error loading config {config_file}: {e}\")\n                else:\n                    # Branch menu - recurse\n                    submenu = self._scan_menu_directory(item_path)\n                    if submenu:\n                        submenu[\"name\"] = item_path.name\n                        submenu[\"type\"] = \"branch\"\n                        items.append(submenu)\n            elif item_path.suffix == '.py' and item_path.name != '__init__.py':\n                # Python script - add as runnable script\n                items.append({\n                    \"name\": item_path.stem,\n                    \"type\": \"script\",\n                    \"path\": str(item_path)\n                })\n        \n        # Return as branch menu if has items\n        if items:\n            return {\n                \"name\": directory.name,\n                \"type\": \"branch\",\n                \"items\": items\n            }\n        \n        return None\n    \n    def _run_script(self, script_path, venv_path=None):\n        \"\"\"Run a Python script, optionally using a virtual environment\"\"\"\n        if not script_path or not os.path.exists(script_path):\n            QMessageBox.critical(\n                self.parent,\n                \"Error\",\n                f\"Script not found:\\n{script_path}\"\n            )\n            return\n        \n        try:\n            # Determine which Python executable to use\n            if venv_path and os.path.exists(os.path.join(venv_path, \"bin\", \"python\")):\n                python_exe = os.path.join(venv_path, \"bin\", \"python\")\n            elif venv_path and os.path.exists(os.path.join(venv_path, \"Scripts\", \"python.exe\")):\n                python_exe = os.path.join(venv_path, \"Scripts\", \"python.exe\")\n            else:\n                python_exe = \"python3\"\n            \n            # Run the script\n            subprocess.Popen([python_exe, script_path], cwd=os.path.dirname(script_path))\n            \n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error running script {script_path}: {e}\")\n            QMessageBox.critical(\n                self.parent,\n                \"Error\",\n                f\"Failed to run script:\\n{e}\"\n            )\n    \n    def _open_leaf_menu(self, menu_data):\n        \"\"\"Show window with content from folder's config.json\"\"\"\n        config = menu_data.get(\"config\", {})\n        path = menu_data.get(\"path\")\n        \n        if not path:\n            return\n        \n        # Use longer delay to let menu close first (prevents freeze)\n        self._pending_dialog = (config, path)\n        QTimer.singleShot(100, self._show_pending_dialog)\n    \n    def _show_pending_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Show the pending dialog\"\"\"\n        if self._pending_dialog:\n            config, path = self._pending_dialog\n            self._show_leaf_dialog(config, path)\n            self._pending_dialog = None\n    \n    def _show_leaf_dialog(self, config, path):\n        \"\"\"Actually show the leaf dialog\"\"\"\n        # Get settings from config\n        content_file = config.get('windowContent', '')\n        script_cmd = config.get('windowScript', '')\n        loop_interval = config.get('loop', 0)\n        looptype = config.get('looptype', 'second')\n        title = config.get('title', path.name)\n        \n        # Create window\n        dialog = QDialog(self.parent)\n        dialog.setWindowTitle(title)\n        dialog.setGeometry(100, 100, 600, 400)\n        \n        # Dark theme for all leaf menus\n        if script_cmd:\n            # Terminal-like for script output\n            dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QDialog { background-color: #1a1a1a; }\n                QTextEdit { \n                    background-color: #000000; \n                    color: #00ff00; \n                    border: 1px solid #333333;\n                    border-radius: 3px;\n                    padding: 10px;\n                    font-family: monospace;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                }\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: #ffffff;\n                    border: 1px solid #555555;\n                    padding: 10px 20px;\n                    border-radius: 4px;\n                    font-weight: bold;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #5a5a5a;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n        else:\n            # Dark theme for static content\n            dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QDialog { background-color: #1a1a1a; }\n                QTextEdit { \n                    background-color: #2d2d2d; \n                    color: #ffffff; \n                    border: 1px solid #444444;\n                    border-radius: 3px;\n                    padding: 10px;\n                    font-family: monospace;\n                    font-size: 12px;\n                }\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: #ffffff;\n                    border: 1px solid #555555;\n                    padding: 10px 20px;\n                    border-radius: 4px;\n\n[-- File truncated: 4154 chars remaining (34.2%). Initial run\n            update_output()\n        else:\n            # Static content\n            if content_file:\n                full_path = path / content_file\n                try:\n                    with open(full_path, 'r') as f:\n                        text.setText(f.read())\n                except FileNotFoundError:\n                    text.setText(f\"Content not found: {content_file}\")\n                except Exception as e:\n                    text.setText(f\"Error reading content: {e}\")\n        \n        dialog.show()\n        dialog.raise_()\n        dialog.activateWindow()\n    \n    def _run_ok_script(self, window, script_path):\n        \"\"\"Run ok.py script and close window\"\"\"\n        try:\n            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"ok_script\", str(script_path))\n            module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n            spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n            if hasattr(module, 'run'):\n                module.run(window)\n            else:\n                window.accept()\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error running ok.py: {e}\")\n            window.accept()\n    \n    def _populate_menu(self, menu, items):\n        \"\"\"Populate a menu with items\"\"\"\n        for item in items:\n            if item.get(\"type\") == \"separator\":\n                menu.addSeparator()\n            elif item.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                #
