<!-- {"role": "assistant", "content": "<WriteFile>\n<fileName>src/panel_pyqt5.py</fileName>\n<contentOfFile>#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nPanel widget for KosDWM PyQt5 version\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import (\n    QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, \n    QComboBox, QFrame, QMenu, QAction, QButtonGroup\n)\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\nfrom PyQt5.QtGui import QFont\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfrom src.panel_api_pyqt5 import PanelAPI\n\n\nclass Panel(QFrame):\n    \"\"\"\n    Top panel with gadgets, menus, clock, and desktop switcher\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        # Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        # Initialize Panel API server (before setup_ui so gadgets can access it)\n        self.api = PanelAPI(port=8080)\n        self.api.start()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager and store as instance variable for config access\n        if not hasattr(self, 'gadget_manager') or self.gadget_manager is None:\n            self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Debug: print how many gadgets we're loading\n        enabled = self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Loading {len(enabled)} gadgets\")\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets - pass panel reference so gadgets can access API\n        for gadget in enabled:\n            print(f\"[Panel] Adding gadget: {gadget.get_name()} with icon {gadget.get_icon()}\")\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Make button visible and styled\n            btn.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 8px;\n                    font-size: 14px;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #555555;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications and API access\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n        \n        # Force layout update\n        self.gadgets_frame.update()\n        self.gadgets_frame.show()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Gadgets loaded: {list(self._gadget_buttons.keys())}\")\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        print(f\"[Panel] build_menus_menu called\")\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        menus = self.menu_manager.load_menus()\n        print(f\"[Panel] Loaded {len(menus)} menus from menu_manager\")\n        \n        for menu_data in menus:\n            menu_name = menu_data.get(\"name\")\n            menu_type = menu_data.get(\"type\")\n            print(f\"[Panel] Processing menu: {menu_name} (type={menu_type})\")\n            \n            if menu_type == \"xdgmenumaker\":\n                # xdgmenumaker generates its own submenu\n                print(f\"[Panel]  Adding xdgmenumaker menu: {menu_name}\")\n                xdg_menu = self.menu_manager._generate_xdg_menu(menu_data)\n                # Add as submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_name)\n                # Copy actions from xdg_menu to submenu\n                for action in xdg_menu.actions():\n                    if action.menu():\n                        new_submenu = submenu.addMenu(action.text())\n                        for subaction in action.menu().actions():\n                            new_submenu.addAction(subaction)\n                    elif action.isSeparator():\n                        submenu.addSeparator()\n                    else:\n                        submenu.addAction(action)\n            elif menu_type == \"script\":\n                # Script menu\n                print(f\"[Panel]  Adding script menu: {menu_name}\")\n                action = menu.addAction(menu_name)\n                script_path = menu_data.get(\"path\", \"\")\n                venv_path = menu_data.get(\"venv\", \"\")\n                action.triggered.connect(\n                    lambda checked, p=script_path, v=venv_path: self.menu_manager._run_script(p, v)\n                )\n            elif menu_type == \"leaf\":\n                # Leaf menu\n                print(f\"[Panel]  Adding leaf menu: {menu_name}\")\n                action = menu.addAction(menu_name)\n                action.triggered.connect(\n                    lambda checked, m=menu_data: self.menu_manager._open_leaf_menu(m)\n                )\n            elif menu_type == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n                print(f\"[Panel]  Added branch menu: {menu_name}\")\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        # Connect signal to reload menus when changes are saved\n        dialog.menus_changed.connect(self.reload_menus)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def reload_menus(self):\n        \"\"\"Reload menus from disk and refresh the menu button\"\"\"\n        # Clear and rebuild the menus menu\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def closeEvent(self, event):\n        \"\"\"Clean up on close - stop the API server\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, 'api') and self.api:\n            self.api.stop()\n        event.accept()\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 338, "rowId": 241, "timestamp": 1784983091.5427036, "date": "2026-07-25", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 101043, "response_tokens": 4055} -->

Scan each directory in Menus as a top-level menu\n        for menu_dir in sorted(self.menus_dir.iterdir()):\n            if menu_dir.is_dir() and menu_dir.name != '__pycache__':\n                print(f\"[MenuManager] Scanning menu directory: {menu_dir.name}\")\n                menu_data = self._scan_menu_directory(menu_dir)\n                if menu_data:\n                    item_count = len(menu_data.get('items', []))\n                    print(f\"[MenuManager] Loaded menu: {menu_data.get('name')} with {item_count} items\")\n                    menus.append(menu_data)\n        \n        print(f\"[MenuManager] Total menus loaded: {len(menus)}\")\n        return menus\n    \n    def _scan_menu_directory(self, directory):\n        \"\"\"Recursively scan a menu directory\"\"\"\n        items = []\n        \n        print(f\"[MenuManager] _scan_menu_directory: {directory}\")\n        \n        for item_path in sorted(directory.iterdir()):\n            if item_path.name == '__pycache__':\n                continue\n            \n            if item_path.is_dir():\n                print(f\"[MenuManager]  Found dir: {item_path.name}\")\n                # Check if it's a script type config\n                        if menu_type == \"script\":\n                            # Script menu with config\n                            script_path = config.get(\"scriptPath\", \"\")\n                            venv_path = config.get(\"venvPath\", \"\")\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"script\",\n                                \"path\": script_path,\n                                \"venv\": venv_path,\n                                \"config\": config\n                            })\n                            print(f\"[MenuManager]   Added script item: {item_path.name}\")\n                        elif menu_type == \"xdgmenumaker\":\n                            # xdgmenumaker - generates menu from XDG .desktop files\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"xdgmenumaker\",\n                                \"config\": config,\n                                \"path\": item_path\n                            })\n                            print(f\"[MenuManager]   Added xdgmenumaker item: {item_path.name}\")\n                        else:\n\n[-- File truncated: 16538 chars remaining (80.7%).  \"\"\"\n    Manages dynamic menus for KosDWM - auto-generated from directory structure\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.menus_dir =          print(f\"[MenuManager]  Added script menu: {menu_dir.name}\")\n                            continue\n                        elif menu_type == \"leaf\":\n                            menus.append({\n                                \"name\": menu_dir.name,\n                                \"type\": \"leaf\",\n                                \"config\": config,\n          except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"[MenuManager]  Error loading top-level config {config_file}: {e}\")\n                \n                # Otherwise, scan for menus loaded: {len(menus)}\")\n        return menus\n    \n    def _scan_menu_directory(self, directory):\n        \"\"\"Recursively scan a menu directory\"\"\"\n        items = []\n        \n        print(f\"[MenuManager] _scan_menu_directory: {directory}\")\n        \n        for item_path in sorted(directory.iterdir()):\n            if item_path.name == '__pycache__':\n                continue\n            \n            if item_path.is_dir():\n                print(f\"[MenuManager]  Found dir: {item_path.name}\")\n                # Check if this subfolder has config.json\n config = json.load(f)\n                        \n                        menu_type = config.get(\"type\", \"leaf\")\n                        print(f\"[MenuManager]   Config found, items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"script\",\n                                \"path\": script_path,\n                                \"venv\": venv_path,\n                                \"config\": config\n                            })\n                            print(f\"[MenuManager]   Added script item: {item_path.name}\")\n                        elif menu_type == \"xdgmenumaker\":\n                            # xdgmenumaker - generates menu from XDG .desktop files\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"xdgmenumaker\",\n                                \"config\": config,\n                                \"path\": item_path\n                            })\n                            print(f\"[MenuManager]   Added xdgmenumaker item: {item_path.name}\")\n                        else:\n                            # Leaf menu - add as clickable item\n                            items.append({\n                                \"name\": item_path.name,\n                                \"type\": \"leaf\",\n                                \"config\": config,\n                                \"path\": item_path\n                            })\n                            print(f\"[MenuManager]   Added leaf item: {item_path.name}\")\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"[MenuManager]   Error loading config {config_file}: {e}\")\n                else:\n                    # Branch menu - recurse\n                    print(f\"[MenuManager]   No config, recursing as branch\")\n                    submenu = def _run_script(self, script_path, venv_path=None):\n        \"\"\"Run a Python script, optionally using a virtual environment\"\"\"\n        print(f\"[MenuManager] _run_script: {script_path},           self.parent,\n                \"Error\",\n                f\"Failed to run script:\\n{e}\"\n            )\n    \n    def _open_leaf_menu(self, menu_data):\n        \"\"\"Show window with content from folder's config.json\"\"\"\n        config = menu_data.get(\"config\", {})\n        path = menu_data.get(\"path\")\n        \n        if not path:\n            return\n        \n        # Use longer delay to let menu close first (prevents freeze)\n        self._pending_dialog = (config, path)\n        QTimer.singleShot(100, self._show_pending_dialog)\n    \n    def _show_pending_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Show the pending dialog\"\"\"\n        if self._pending_dialog:\n            config, path = self._pending_dialog\n            self._show_leaf_dialog(config, path)\n            self._pending_dialog = None\n    \n    def _show_leaf_dialog(self, config, path):\n        \"\"\"Actually show the leaf dialog\"\"\"\n        # Get settings from config\n        content_file = config.get('windowContent', '')\n        script_cmd = config.get('windowScript', '')\n        loop_interval = config.get('loop', 0)\n        looptype = config.get('looptype', 'second')\n        title = config.get('title', path.name)\n        \n        # Create window\n        dialog = QDialog(self.parent)\n        dialog.setWindowTitle(title)\n        dialog.setGeometry(100, 100, 600, 400)\n        \n        # Dark theme for all leaf menus\n        if script_cmd:\n            # Terminal-like for script output\n            dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QDialog { background-color: #1a1a1a; }\n                QTextEdit { \n                    background-color: #000000; \n                    color: #00ff00; \n                    border: 1px solid #333333;\n                    border-radius: 3px;\n                    padding: 10px;\n                    font-family: monospace;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                }\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: #ffffff;\n                    border: 1px solid #555555;\n                    padding: 10px 20px;\n                    border-radius: 4px;\n                    font-weight: bold;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #5a5a5a;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n        else:\n            # Dark theme for static content\n            dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QDialog { background-color: #1a1a1a; }\n                QTextEdit { \n                    background-color: #2d2d2d; \n                    color: #ffffff; \n                    border: 1px solid #444444;\n                    border-radius: 3px;\n                    padding: 10px;\n                    font-family: monospace;\n                    font-size: 12px;\n                }\n                QPushButton {\n                    background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                    color: #ffffff;\n                    border: 1px solid #555555;\n                    padding: 10px 20px;\n                    border-radius: 4px;\n                    font-weight: bold;\n                }\n                QPushButton:hover {\n                    background-color: #5a5a5a;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n        \n        layout = QVBoxLayout(dialog)\n        layout.setSpacing(10)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10)\n        \n        # Text widget\n        text = QTextEdit()\n        text.setReadOnly(True)\n        layout.addWidget(text)\n        \n        # Button frame\n        btn_frame = QHBoxLayout()\n        btn_frame.addStretch()\n        \n        # Check for ok.py\n        ok_script = path / \"ok.py\"\n        if ok_script.exists():\n            ok_btn = QPushButton(\"OK\")\n            ok_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._run_ok_script(dialog, ok_script))\n            btn_frame.addWidget(ok_btn)\n        else:\n            ok_btn = QPushButton(\"OK\")\n            ok_btn.clicked.connect(dialog.accept)\n            btn_frame.addWidget(ok_btn)\n        \n        layout.addLayout(btn_frame)\n        \n        # Handle script command (looping)\n        if script_cmd:\n            interval_ms = loop_interval * 1000 if looptype == 'second' else loop_interval\n            \n            def update_output():\n                try:\n                    result = subprocess.run(\n                        script_cmd, \n                        shell=True, \n                        capture_output=True, \n                        text=True, \n                        timeout=10\n                    )\n                    text.clear()\n                    text.setText(result.stdout if result.stdout else result.stderr)\n                except Exception as e:\n                    text.clear()\n                    text.setText(str(e))\n                \n                # Schedule next update if window still exists and looping enabled\n                if dialog.isVisible() and loop_interval > 0:\n                    QTimer.singleShot(interval_ms, update_output)\n            \n            # Initial run\n            update_output()\n        else:\n            # Static content\n            if content_file:\n                full_path = path / content_file\n                try:\n                    with open(full_path, 'r') as f:\n                        text.setText(f.read())\n                except FileNotFoundError:\n                    text.setText(f\"Content not found: {content_file}\")\n                except Exception as e:\n                    text.setText(f\"Error reading content: {e}\")\n        \n        dialog.show()\n        dialog.raise_()\n        dialog.activateWindow()\n    \n    def _run_ok_script(self, window, script_path):\n        \"\"\"Run ok.py script and close window\"\"\"\n        try:\n            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"ok_script\", str(script_path))\n            module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n            spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n            if hasattr(module, 'run'):\n                module.run(window)\n            else:\n                window.accept()\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[MenuManager] Error running ok.py: {e}\")\n            window.accept()\n    \n    def _generate_xdg_menu(self, menu_data):\n        \"\"\"Generate menu from XDG .desktop files like OpenBox\"\"\"\n        print(f\"[MenuManager] _generate_xdg_menu called for: {menu_data.get('name')}\")\n        config = menu_data.get(\"config\", {})\n        path = menu_data.get(\"path\")\n        \n        # Create a menu that will be populated with XDG apps\n        menu = QMenu(self.parent)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Get categories to organize apps\n        categories = {}\n        \n        # Scan XDG application directories\n        xdg_dirs = [\n Scanning XDG dirs: {xdg_dirs}\")\n        \n        for xdg_dir in xdg_dirs:\n            if not xdg_dir.exists():\n                print(f\"[MenuManager]   Skipping non-existent: {xdg_dir}\")\n                continue\n            \n            desktop_files = list(xdg_dir.glob(\"*.desktop\"))\n            print(f\"[MenuManager]   Found {len(desktop_files)} .desktop files in {xdg_dir}\")\n            \n            for desktop_file in desktop_files:\n                try:\n                    app_info = self._parse_desktop_file(desktop_file)\n                    if app_info and app_info.get(\"NoDisplay\") != \"true\":\n                        category = app_info.get(\"Categories\", \"Other\").split(\";\")[0] or \"Other\"\n                        if category not in categories:\n                            categories[category] = []\n                        categories[category].append(app_info)\n                        print(f\"[MenuManager]    Added app: {app_info.get('Name')} in {category}\")\n                    else:\n                        print(f\"[MenuManager]    Skipped (NoDisplay): {desktop_file.name}\")\n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"[MenuManager]    Error parsing {desktop_file}: {e}\")\n        \n        print(f\"[MenuManager]  Categories found: {list(categories.keys())}\")\n        \n        # Sort categories and add to menu\n        for category in sorted(categories.keys()):\n            apps = categories[category]\n            apps.sort(key=lambda x: x.get(\"Name\", \"\").lower())\n            \n            submenu = menu.addMenu(category)\n            print(f\"[MenuManager]  Added category: {category} with {len(apps)} apps\")\n            for app in apps:\n                action = submenu.addAction(app.get(\"Name\", \"Unknown\"))\n                action.setIcon(self.parent.style().standardIcon(self.parent.style().SP_ComputerIcon))\n                action.triggered.connect(lambda checked, a=app: self._launch_xdg_app(a))\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def _parse_desktop_file(self, desktop_path):\n        \"\"\"Parse a .desktop file and return app info\"\"\"\n        info = {}\n        with open(desktop_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\n            in_entry = False\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if line == \"[Desktop Entry]\":\n                    in_entry = True\n                    continue\n                if line.startswith('[') and line.endswith(']'):\n                    in_entry = False\n                    continue\n                if in_entry and '=' in line:\n                    key, value = line.split('=', 1)\n                    info[key] = value\n        return info\n    \n    def _launch_xdg_app(self, app_info):\n        \"\"\"Launch an XDG application\"\"\"\n        print(f\"[MenuManager] _launch_xdg_app: {app_info.get('Name')}\")\n        try:\n            exec_cmd = app_info.get(\"Exec\", \"\")\n            if exec_cmd:\n                # Remove field codes like %f, %F, %u, %U, etc.\n                exec_cmd = re.sub(r'%[fFuUdDnNickvm]', '', exec_cmd).strip()\n                \n                # Run in terminal if needed\n                if app_info.get(\"Terminal\") == \"true\":\n                    print(f\"[MenuManager]  Running in terminal: xterm -e {exec_cmd}\")\n                    subprocess.Popen([\"xterm\", \"-e\", exec_cmd])\n                else:\n                    print(f\"[MenuManager]  Running: {exec_cmd}\")\n                    subprocess.Popen(exec_cmd, shell=True)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[MenuManager] Error launching app: {e}\")\n            QMessageBox.critical(\n                self.parent,\n                \"Error\",\n                f\"Failed to launch {app_info.get('Name', 'application')}:\\n{e}\"\n            )\n    \n    def _populate_menu(self, menu, items):\n        \"\"\"Populate a menu with items\"\"\"\n        print(f\"[MenuManager] _populate_menu with {len(items)} items\")\n        for item in items:\n            item_type = item.get(\"type\")\n            item_name = item.get(\"name\")\n            print(f\"[MenuManager]  Processing: {item_name} (type={item_type})\")\n            \n            if item_type == \"separator\":\n                menu.addSeparator()\n            elif item_type == \"branch\":\n                # Create nested submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(item_name)\n                self._populate_menu(submenu, item.get(\"items\", []))\n            elif item_type == \"xdgmenumaker\":\n                # Generate XDG menu dynamically\n                print(f\"[MenuManager]   Generating xdgmenumaker for: {item_name}\")\n                xdg_menu = self._generate_xdg_menu(item)\n                # Copy actions from xdg_menu to current menu\n                for action in xdg_menu.actions():\n                    if action.menu():\n                        # It's a submenu (category)\n                        new_submenu = action.isSeparator():\n                        menu.addSeparator()\n                    else:\n                        menu.addAction(action)\n                print(f\"[MenuManager]   xdgmenumaker populated with {len(xdg_menu.actions())} actions\")\n            elif item_type == \"script\":\n                # Python script - run with optional venv\n                action = menu.addAction(item_name)\n                script_path = item.get(\"path\", \"\")\n                venv_path = item.get(\"venv\", \"\")\n                action.triggered.connect(\n > 
> ## Key Commands
> - **TkInter Version**: `python run.py`
> - **PyQt5 Version**: `python main_pyqt5.py`
> - Config: Edit `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`
> - Menus: Create folders in `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`
> 
> ## Important Files
> 
> ### PyQt5 Version (Current)
> - Entry point: `main_pyqt5.py`
> - Panel: `src/panel_pyqt5.py`
> - Menus: `src/menus_pyqt5.py`

> - Window Manager: `src/window_manager_pyqt5.py`
> - Desktop Manager: `src/desktop_manager_pyqt5.py`
> - Panel ...

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "# KosDWM Agent Guide\n\n## Key Commands\n- **TkInter Version**: `python run.py`\n- **PyQt5 Version**: `python main_pyqt5.py`\n- Config: Edit `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`\n- Menus: Create folders in `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`\n\n## Important Files\n\n### PyQt5 Version (Current)\n- Entry point: `main_pyqt5.py`\n- Panel: `src/panel_pyqt5.py`\n- Menus: `src/menus_pyqt5.py`\n- Gadgets: `src/gadgets_pyqt5.py`\n- Window Manager: `src/window_manager_pyqt5.py`\n- Desktop Manager: `src/desktop_manager_pyqt5.py`\n- Panel API: `src/panel_api_pyqt5.py`\n- About Dialog: `src/about_dialog_pyqt5.py`\n- Notices Gadget: `src/notices_gadget_pyqt5.py`\n\n### Legacy TkInter Version\n- Entry point: `run.py`\n- Config manager: `src/config.py`\n- Helper functions: `src/functions.py`\n\n## Configuration Locations\n- Main config: `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`\n- Gadgets config: `~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets.json`\n- Menus directory: `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`\n- Notices data: `~/.config/KosDWM/notices.json`\n\n## PyQt5 Features\n\n### Window Switcher\n- Dropdown showing running windows (wmctrl)\n- Shows desktop numbers for windows on other desktops\n- Visual feedback on window activation\n\n### Auto-Generative Menus\n- Dynamic menus from directory structure\n  - Folders = submenus\n  - `config.json` = leaf menus (content windows)\n  - `.py` files = runnable scripts with `run()` function\n  - `windowScript` = looping command output\n\n### Gadget System\n- Clickable icons in panel\n- **Notices Gadget**: Rich text editor with formatting (bold, italic, underline, color, font, size)\n- Gadgets can register HTTP API endpoints via `panel.api.register()`\n\n### Dark Theme\n- All dialogs use dark background (#1a1a1a) with light text\n- Consistent styling across all configuration dialogs\n\n### About Window\n- Accessible from Config dropdown (\u2699 \u2192 About KosDWM)\n- Shows version from AUTOVERSION.py\n- Credits: B.K., OpenCode contributors\n- System info: Python version, Qt version, Platform\n\n## HTTP Panel API\n\nThe panel runs an HTTP API server on port 8080 (configurable) that allows gadgets to register custom endpoints.\n\n### Default Endpoints\n- `GET /api/status` - API status and endpoint count\n- `GET /api/endpoints` - List all registered endpoints\n\n### Notices Gadget Endpoints\n- `GET /api/notices` - List all notices\n- `POST /api/notices` - Create new notice\n- `POST /api/notices/delete` - Delete notice by ID\n\n### Creating Gadget API Endpoints\n\nGadgets can register HTTP endpoints by overriding `set_panel()`:\n\n```python\nclass MyGadget(GadgetBase):\n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        # Register endpoints when panel is set\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/status\", self._api_status)\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/action\", self._api_action, methods=[\"POST\"])\n    \n    def _api_status(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Handle GET requests\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"gadget\": self.get_name(),\n            \"status\": \"active\",\n            \"method\": request_data.get('method')\n        }\n    \n    def _api_action(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Handle POST requests\"\"\"\n        body = request_data.get('body', {})\n        # Process body data...\n        return {\"result\": \"success\"}\n```\n\n### Request Data Structure\n\nHandler functions receive a dictionary with:\n- `method`: HTTP method (\"GET\", \"POST\", etc.)\n- `path`: Request path\n- `query`: Query parameters dict\n- `headers`: Request headers dict\n- `body`: JSON body (for POST requests)\n\n### Testing with curl\n\n```bash\n# Check API status\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/status | python -m json.tool\n\n# List all notices\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/notices | python -m json.tool\n\n# Create a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"title\":\"Meeting\",\"content\":\"<b>Team meeting</b>\",\"priority\":\"high\"}' \\\n  | python -m json.tool\n\n# Delete a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices/delete \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"id\":\"NOTICE-UUID-HERE\"}' \\\n\n[-- File truncated: 1449 chars remaining (26.6%). > 
> A dynamic window manager with Python-based gadget system. Now with PyQt5!

> ## Features
> - **Dynamic Window Management** - Tiling window manager for X11

Now with PyQt5!\n\n## Features\n- **Dynamic Window Management** - Tiling window manager for X11\n- **PyQt5 Panel** - Modern Qt-based panel with gadgets and menus\n- **Auto-Generative Menus** - Dynamic menus from `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/` directory structure\n  - Folders = submenus\n  - `config.json` = leaf menus with content windows\n  - `.py` scripts = runnable with `run()` function\n  - `windowScript` = looping command output (like `lsof`)\n- **Gadget System** - Pluggable Python gadgets for the panel\n  - **Notices Gadget** - Rich text editor with formatting (bold, italic, underline, font, size, color)\n  - Double-click to edit notices\n  - HTML content persistence\n- **Window Switcher** - Dropdown to switch between running windows (wmctrl)\n- **Notices & Reminders** - Built-in notification system with HTTP API\n- **Dark Theme** - Consistent dark styling across all dialogs\n- **Auto-Versioning** - Automatic version increment on commits\n## Quick Start\n\n```bash\n# Install PyQt5 version\npython install_kosdwm_pyqt5.py\n\n# Run\npython main_pyqt5.py\n```\n\n## Configuration\n\n### Panel Config (`~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`):\n```json\n{\n    \"active_button_bg\": \"#4a90d9\",\n    \"inactive_button_bg\": \"#606060\",\n    \"bar_height\": 30,\n    \"layout_mode\": \"comboboxes\"\n}\n```\n\n### Menu Structure (`~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`):\n```\nMenus/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 Home/\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 About/              # Leaf menu (config.json + content.html)\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json     # {\"title\": \"About\", \"windowContent\": \"content.html\"}\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 content.html    # Static content\n\u2502   \u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py           # Optional: run() function called on OK\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 Test/               # Another leaf menu\n\u2502       \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json     # {\"windowScript\": \"lsof -i\", \"loop\": 5}\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 Scripts/\n    \u2514\u2500\u2500 myscript.py         # Runnable script with run() function\n```\n\n### Gadget Config (`~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets.json`):\n```json\n{\n    \"enabled\": [\"hello_world\", \"test_gadget\", \"notices\"]\n}\n```\n\n## Documentation\n\n- [Notices API](NOTICES_API_DOCUMENTATION.md) - <!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nInstall Git Hooks for KosDWM Auto-Versioning\n============================================\n\nInstalls the pre-commit hook that automatically increments version numbers.\n\nUsage:\n    python install_hooks.py\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport stat\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\nHOOK_CONTENT = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Pre-commit hook: auto-increment version in AUTOVERSION.py + update CHANGELOG.md\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sys\nimport subprocess\nimport os\nfrom datetime import date\n\nREPO_ROOT = subprocess.run(\n    ['git', 'rev-parse', '--show-toplevel'],\n    capture_output=True, text=True\n).stdout.strip()\n\nVERSION_FILE = os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'AUTOVERSION.py')\nCHANGELOG_FILE = os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'CHANGELOG.md')\n\n# Skip on merge commits\nmerge_msg = os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, '.git', 'MERGE_MSG')\nif os.path.exists(merge_msg):\n    with open(merge_msg) as f:\n        if 'Merge' in f.read():\n            sys.exit(0)\n\nif not os.path.exists(VERSION_FILE):\n    sys.exit(0)\n\nwith open(VERSION_FILE) as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\nmatch = re.search(r'VERSION\\\\s*=\\\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"', content)\nif not match:\n    sys.exit(0)\n\ncurrent = match.group(1)\nparts = current.split('.')\n\nif len(parts) == 3:\n    new = \"{}.{}.{}\".format(parts[0], parts[1], int(parts[2]) + 1)\nelif len(parts) == 2:\n    new = \"{}.{}.1\".format(parts[0], parts[1])\nelse:\n    sys.exit(0)\n\nresult = subprocess.run(\n    ['git', 'diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=ACDMR'],\n    capture_output=True, text=True\n)\nchanged_files = [f for f in result.stdout.strip().split('\\\\n') if f]\n\ncontent = content.replace('VERSION = \"{}\"'.format(current), 'VERSION = \"{}\"'.format(new))\nwith open(VERSION_FILE, 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\n\nprint(f\"Version: {current} -> {new}\")\n\ntoday = date.today().isoformat()\nfiles_str = ', '.join(changed_files[:5])\nif len(changed_files) > 5:\n    files_str += f\" +{len(changed_files) - 5}\"\n\nnew_entry = f\"\"\"## [{new}] - {today}\n\n### Changed\n- Auto-incremented from {current}\n\n[-- File truncated: 1822 chars remaining (47.8%). Use <ReadFile><fileName>install_hooks.py</fileName><max_chars>1822</max_chars></ReadFile> to read further. --]", "sessionId": 338, "rowId": 244, "timestamp": 1784983485.9675379, "date": "2026-07-25", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

Branch Menu (Submenu)\nEmpty folder containing other folders. Creates a cascading submenu.\n\n#### 2. Leaf Menu (Content Window)\nFolder with `config.json`:\n```json\n{\n    \"title\": \"System Info\",\n    \"windowContent\": \"content.html\",\n    \"windowScript\": \"lsof -i\",\n    \"loop\": 5,\n    \"looptype\": \"second\"\n}\n```\n\n#### 3. Script Menu\nFolder with `config.json`:\n```json\n{\n    \"type\": \"script\",\n    \"scriptPath\": \"/path/to/script.py\",\n    \"venvPath\": \"/home/user/.venv/myenv\"\n}\n```\n- Runs script with specified Python interpreter\n- Supports virtual environments (auto-detects `bin/python` or `Scripts/python.exe`)\n\n#### 4. xdgmenumaker Menu\nFolder with `config.json`:\n```json\n{\n    \"type\": \"xdgmenumaker\",\n    \"icon\": \"applications-other\",\n    \"terminal\": false\n}\n```\n- Auto-generates menu from XDG `.desktop` files\n- Organized by categories (Accessories, Development, Games, Graphics, Network, Office, etc.)\n- Scans: `/usr/share/applications`, `/usr/local/share/applications`, `~/.local/share/applications`\n\n### Menu Configuration Dialog\nAccess via: Panel \u2192 \u2699 Config \u2192 Menu Settings\n\nFeatures:\n- Tree view of all menus\n- Edit menu properties (Name, Type)\n- Type-specific configuration:\n  - **Leaf**: Window Title, Content File, Window Script, Loop Interval\n  - **Script**: Script Path, Virtual Environment Path\n  - **xdgmenumaker**: Icon, Terminal checkbox\n- Create new menus/folders\n- Delete menus\n- Live preview for Leaf menus\n- Auto-reload panel menu after saving\n\n### Gadget System\n- Clickable icons in panel\n- **Notices Gadget**: Rich text editor with formatting (bold, italic, underline, color, font, size)\n- Gadgets can register HTTP API endpoints via `panel.api.register()`\n\n### Dark Theme\n- All dialogs use dark background (#1a1a1a) with light text\n- Consistent styling across all configuration dialogs\n\n### About Window\n- Accessible from Config dropdown (\u2699 \u2192 About KosDWM)\n- Shows version from AUTOVERSION.py\n- Credits: B.K., OpenCode contributors\n- System info: Python version, Qt version, Platform\n\n## HTTP Panel API\n\nThe panel runs an HTTP API server on port 8080 (configurable) that allows gadgets to register custom endpoints.\n\n### Default Endpoints\n- `GET /api/status` - API status and endpoint count\n- `GET /api/endpoints` - List all registered endpoints\n\n### Notices Gadget Endpoints\n- `GET /api/notices` - List all notices\n- `POST /api/notices` - Create new notice\n- `POST /api/notices/delete` - Delete notice by ID\n\n### Creating Gadget API Endpoints\n\nGadgets can register HTTP endpoints by overriding `set_panel()`:\n\n```python\nclass MyGadget(GadgetBase):\n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        # Register endpoints when panel is set\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/status\", self._api_status)\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/action\", self._api_action, methods=[\"POST\"])\n    \n    def _api_status(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Handle GET requests\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"gadget\": self.get_name(),\n            \"status\": \"active\",\n            \"method\": request_data.get('method')\n        }\n    \n    def _api_action(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Handle POST requests\"\"\"\n        body = request_data.get('body', {})\n        # Process body data...\n        return {\"result\": \"success\"}\n```\n\n### Request Data Structure\n\nHandler functions receive a dictionary with:\n- `method`: HTTP method (\"GET\", \"POST\", etc.)\n- `path`: Request path\n- `query`: Query parameters dict\n- `headers`: Request headers dict\n- `body`: JSON body (for POST requests)\n\n### Testing with curl\n\n```bash\n# Check API status\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/status | python -m json.tool\n\n# List all notices\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/notices | python -m json.tool\n\n# Create a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"title\":\"Meeting\",\"content\":\"<b>Team meeting</b>\",\"priority\":\"high\"}' \\\n  | python -m json.tool\n\n# Delete a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices/delete \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"id\":\"NOTICE-UUID-HERE\"}' \\\n  | python -m json.tool\n```\n\n## Menu Structure Examples\n\n### Example 1: Mixed Menu Types\n```\nMenus/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 Home/                      # Branch menu (submenu)\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 About/                 # Leaf menu\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json\n\u2502   \u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 content.html\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 System/                # Leaf menu with script\n\u2502       \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json        # {\"windowScript\": \"df -h\", \"loop\": 5}\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 KosPrograms/               # Branch menu\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 KosFM/                 # Script menu\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 config.json        # {\"type\": \"script\", \"scriptPath\": \"...\", \"venvPath\": \"...\"}\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 OtherPrograms/             # xdgmenumaker menu\n    \u2514\u2500\u2500 config.json            # {\"type\": \"xdgmenumaker\"}\n```\n\n## Debugging\n\nRun with debug mode to see detailed logs:\n```bash\npython main_pyqt5.py -d\n```\n\nLook for `[MenuManager]` and `[Panel]` debug messages to trace menu loading.\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 338, "rowId": 244, "timestamp": 1784983501.7121847, "date": "2026-07-25", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 119277, "response_tokens": 1881} -->

2025-01-22\n\n### Added\n- **Menu Configuration Dialog** (`src/menu_config_pyqt5.py`) - GUI for managing menus\n  - Tree view of all menus with create/delete/rename\n  - Support for 4 menu types: Branch, Leaf, Script, xdgmenumaker\n  - Type-specific configuration fields\n  - Live preview for Leaf menus\n  - Auto-reload panel menu after saving (via `menus_changed` signal)\n- **Script Menu Type** - Execute Python scripts with optional virtual environment\n  - `scriptPath`: Path to Python script\n  - `venvPath`: Path to virtual environment (auto-detects Linux/Windows)\n  - Runs with `subprocess.Popen()` for independent execution\n- **xdgmenumaker Menu Type** - Auto-generate menu from XDG .desktop files\n  - Scans `/usr/share/applications`, `/usr/local/share/applications`, `~/.local/share/applications`\n  - Organizes apps by categories (Accessories, Development, Games, Graphics, Network, Office, etc.)\n  - Parses .desktop files for Name, Exec, Categories, Terminal, NoDisplay\n  - Handles field codes (%f, %F, %u, %U, etc.)\n  - Supports Terminal=true apps (runs in xterm)\n- **Enhanced MenuManager** (`src/menus_pyqt5.py`)\n  - `_run_script()` with venv support\n  - `_generate_xdg_menu()` for xdgmenumaker\n  - `_parse_desktop_file()` for .desktop parsing\n  - `_launch_xdg_app()` with terminal detection\n  - Comprehensive debug logging\n\n### Changed\n- **Panel Menu Building** - Now handles all menu types at top level\n- **Menu Type Detection** - Top-level directories with config.json are now treated as menu items\n- **Leaf Menu Type** - Now properly detected via `type: \"leaf\"` in config.json\n\n### Fixed\n- **Menu Reload** - Panel now reloads menus after configuration changes via signal connection\n\n## [1.1.0] - 2026-07-23\n\n### Added\n- **Rich Text Editor for Notices** - Full formatting support (bold, italic, underline, font, size, color)\n- **Double-click to Edit Notices** - Quick edit by double-clicking any notice\n- **HTML Content Persistence** - Rich text survives save/load cycles\n\n### Fixed\n- **Notices Disappearing** - Fixed dialog freezing issue when clicking notices\n- **Dark Theme Consistency** - All notices dialogs now use proper dark theme\n\n### Changed\n- Notices list now shows content preview (HTML stripped for display)\n- Notice detail view renders HTML with full formatting\n\n## [1.0.8] - > <contentOfFile># KosDWM
> 
> A dynamic window manager with Python-based gadget system. Now with PyQt5!
> 
> ## Features
> - **Dynamic Window Management** - Tiling window manager for X11
> - **PyQt5 Panel** - Modern Qt-based panel with gadgets and menus
> - **Auto-Generative Menus** - Dynamic menus from `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/` directory structure
>   - **Branch** (folders) = submenus containing other menus
>   - **Leaf** (`config.json`) = content windows with HTML or lo...

> </contentOfFile>
> </WriteFile>

<!-- {"role": "assistant", "content": "<WriteFile>\n<fileName>README.md</fileName>\n<contentOfFile># KosDWM\n\nA dynamic window manager with Python-based gadget system. Now with PyQt5!\n\n## Features\n- **Dynamic Window Management** - Tiling window manager for X11\n- **PyQt5 Panel** - Modern Qt-based panel with gadgets and menus\n- **Auto-Generative Menus** - Dynamic menus from `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/` directory structure\n  - **Branch** (folders) = submenus containing other menus\n  - **Leaf** (`config.json`) = content windows with HTML or looping command output\n  - **Script** (`config.json` with `type: \"script\"`) = executable Python scripts with optional venv\n  - **xdgmenumaker** (`config.json` with `type: \"xdgmenumaker\"`) = auto-generated XDG application menu\n- **Menu Configuration Dialog** - GUI for creating and managing menus\n- **Gadget System** - Pluggable Python gadgets for the panel\n  - **Notices Gadget** - Rich text editor with formatting (bold, italic, underline, font, size, color)\n  - Double-click to edit notices\n  - HTML content persistence\n- **Window Switcher** - Dropdown to switch between running windows (wmctrl)\n- **Notices & Reminders** - Built-in notification system with HTTP API\n- **Dark Theme** - Consistent dark styling across all dialogs\n- **Auto-Versioning** - Automatic version increment on commits\n\n## Quick Start\n\n```bash\n# Install PyQt5 version\npython install_kosdwm_pyqt5.py\n\n# Run\npython main_pyqt5.py\n\n# Run with debug mode\npython main_pyqt5.py -d\n```\n\n## Menu Types\n\n### 1. Branch Menu (Submenu)\nFolder containing other folders. Creates a cascading submenu.\n\n### 2. Leaf Menu (Content Window)\nFolder with `config.json` showing HTML content or command output:\n```json\n{\n    \"title\": \"System Info\",\n    \"windowContent\": \"content.html\",\n    \"windowScript\": \"lsof -i\",\n    \"loop\": 5,\n    \"looptype\": \"second\"\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Script Menu\nFolder with `config.json` for running Python scripts:\n```json\n{\n    \"type\": \"script\",\n    \"scriptPath\": \"/home/user/scripts/myapp.py\",\n    \"venvPath\": \"/home/user/.venv/myapp\"\n}\n```\n\n### 4. xdgmenumaker Menu\nFolder with `config.json` for auto-generated XDG application menu:\n```json\n{\n    \"type\": \"xdgmenumaker\",\n    \"icon\": \"applications-other\",\n    \"terminal\": false\n}\n```\n- Automatically populated from system `.desktop` files\n- Organized by categories (Accessories, Development, Games, Graphics, Network, Office, etc.)\n\n## Configuration\n\n### Panel Config (`~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`):\n```json\n{\n    \"active_button_bg\": \"#4a90d9\",\n    \"inactive_button_bg\": \"#606060\",\n    \"bar_height\": 30,\n    \"layout_mode\": \"comboboxes\"\n}\n```\n\n### Menu Structure (`~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`):\n```\nMenus/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 Home/                      # Branch menu (submenu)\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 About/                 # Leaf menu\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json        # {\"title\": \"About\", \"windowContent\": \"content.html\"}\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 content.html       # Static content\n\u2502   \u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py              # Optional: run() function called on OK\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 System/                # Leaf menu with script\n\u2502       \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json        # {\"windowScript\": \"df -h\", \"loop\": 5}\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 KosPrograms/               # Branch menu\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 KosFM/                 # Script menu\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 config.json        # {\"type\": \"script\", \"scriptPath\": \"...\", \"venvPath\": \"...\"}\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 OtherPrograms/             # xdgmenumaker menu\n    \u2514\u2500\u2500 config.json            # {\"type\": \"xdgmenumaker\"}\n```\n\n### Gadget Config (`~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets.json`):\n```json\n{\n    \"enabled\": [\"hello_world\", \"test_gadget\", \"notices\"]\n}\n```\n\n## Menu Configuration\n\nAccess the Menu Configuration dialog from the panel:\n1. Click **\u2699 Config** dropdown\n2. Select **Menu Settings**\n3. Use the GUI to:\n   - Create new menus and folders\n   - Set menu type (Branch, Leaf, Script, xdgmenumaker)\n   - Configure type-specific options\n   - Delete menus\n   - Preview Leaf menu content\n\n## Documentation\n\n- [Agent Guide](AGENTS.md) - Developer and agent documentation\n- [Notices API](NOTICES_API_DOCUMENTATION.md) - HTTP API for notices\n- [PyQt5 Readme](PYQT5_README.md) - PyQt5 specific documentation\n- [Changelog](CHANGELOG.md) - Version history\n\n## Version\n\nCurrent version: See `AUTOVERSION.py` or run `python main_pyqt5.py --version`\n\nTo install git hooks for auto-versioning:\n```bash\npython install_hooks.py\n```\n\n## License\n\nMIT License - Created with love by B.K. and OpenCode (editor + BigPickle) and ollama models.\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 338, "rowId": 244, "timestamp": 1784983524.583311, "date": "2026-07-25", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 122326, "response_tokens": 1160} -->
